Motivation: 现有工作多处理分歧点结构较简单的情形，对同一分支值上存在多个分歧点等任意分歧剖型缺乏统一处理；同时研究ζ-正则化行列式在Hurwitz坐标下的依赖结构具有内在价值。

Method: 结合光滑平凡化、 resolvent幂的迹范数变分、与球面锥模型的矩阵比较；用Davies–Gaffney估计提供一致高能控制；用Schiffer双微分与Rauch变分公式识别零能项。

Result: 证明了局部公式 Det_ζ(Δ_{[φ],F}) = C·det Im B·|τ_B|^2·∏_{k=1}^N ρ(z_k,\bar z_k)^{c_k}，其中省略零特征值；B为周期矩阵，τ_B为局部Bergman tau函数，z_k为分支值坐标，ρ(z,\bar z)=4(1+|z|^2)^{-2}，c_k=1/12 Σ_j(n_{kj}-n_{kj}^{-1})。

Conclusion: 常数C>0与Hurwitz坐标无关，在亏格零时det Im B取为1；该公式对任意分歧剖型成立。

Abstract: We study the Friedrichs Laplacian associated with the pullback of the round metric on \(\mb P^1\) by a nonconstant meromorphic function \(\varphi:X\to\mb P^1\) on a compact Riemann surface. For arbitrary ramification profiles, including several ramification points over the same branch value, we prove the local formula \[ \operatorname{Det}_ζ(Δ_{[\varphi],\mc F}) =C\,\det\operatorname{Im}B\,|τ_B|^2 \prod_{k=1}^Nρ(z_k,\overline{z_k})^{c_k}. \] The zero eigenvalue is omitted. Here \(B\) is the period matrix, \(τ_B\) is the local Bergman tau-function, \(z_k\) are the branch-value coordinates, \(ρ(z,\overline z)=4(1+|z|^2)^{-2}\), and \(c_k=\frac1{12}\sum_j(n_{kj}-n_{kj}^{-1})\), where \(n_{kj}\) are the ramification indices over the \(k\)-th branch value. The constant \(C>0\) is independent of the Hurwitz coordinates, and \(\det\operatorname{Im}B\) is taken to be \(1\) in genus zero. The proof combines smooth trivializations and trace-norm variation of resolvent powers with matrix comparison to spherical conic models. The Davies--Gaffney estimate provides the required uniform high-energy control, while the zero-energy terms are identified through the Schiffer bidifferential and Rauch variational formulas.

</details>


### [182] [A stable self-shrinking Möbius bundle in $\mathbb R^4$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19648)
*Kahnrad Braxton,Tang-Kai Lee,Jonathan J. Zhu*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文证明了 $\mathbb{R}^4$ 中具有 Möbius 丛拓扑的嵌入平均曲率流收缩子的线性稳定性，给出了高余维情形下一个非平坦、非球面的稳定收缩子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稳定超曲面收缩子的分类结果表明，稳定收缩子往往受强限制（如为球面等）。本文旨在探究高余维情形是否同样受限，寻找反例以揭示超曲面与高余维之间的本质差异。

Method: 以 $\mathbb{R}^4$ 中拓扑为 Möbius 丛的嵌入平均曲率流收缩子为研究对象，分析其线性稳定性（即收缩子相关稳定性算子的谱性质）。

Result: 证明了该 Möbius 收缩子是线性稳定的，从而得到高余维下非平坦、非球面的稳定收缩子，与稳定超曲面收缩子的分类形成鲜明对比。

Conclusion: 拓扑上，Möbius 收缩子刻画了实爆破（blow-up）的逆转过程，暗示高余维稳定奇点可能在平均曲率流下编码非平凡的拓扑操作。

Abstract: We prove the linear stability of an embedded mean curvature flow shrinker in $\mathbb R^4$ with the topology of the Möbius bundle. This provides a non-flat, non-spherical stable shrinker in higher codimension, in sharp contrast with the classification of stable hypersurface shrinkers. Topologically, the Möbius shrinker models the reversal of a real blow-up, suggesting that stable higher-codimension singularities may encode non-trivial topological operations under mean curvature flow.

</details>


### [183] [Spacetime Positive Mass Theorem with Boundary for Multiple Time Dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19711)
*Changwen Fang*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该文对具有多个时间方向及光滑紧致边界的单端渐近平坦自旋初始数据证明正质量定理，在迹范主导能量条件与第二基本形式两两可交换假设下，得到能量不等式 E ≥ ‖𝒫‖_tr，并证明边界条件的逐点尖锐性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将经典正质量定理推广到带边界的自旋初始数据情形，特别是允许多个辅助时间方向，以刻画边界处混合项对质量下界的影响。

Method: 在仿射能量空间中构造 Dirac–Witten 调和旋量；利用每个边界分支上固定的辅助时间方向确定局部手征条件；通过逼近方法评估质量恒等式，识别残余混合边界项。

Result: 在边界条件 H + tr_Σ k^T + ‖𝒬_T‖_tr ≤ 0 下得到不等式 E ≥ ‖𝒫‖_tr；证明该边界条件对固定投影算子的逐点尖锐性，并给出具有非零混合边界项的两时间数据示例。

Conclusion: 推广了带边自旋流形的正质量定理，揭示了多时间方向下混合边界项在质量不等式中的作用及其最优性。

Abstract: We prove a positive mass theorem for one-ended asymptotically flat spin initial data with multiple time directions and a smooth compact boundary. Assuming the trace-norm dominant energy condition and pairwise commutativity of the second fundamental forms, a fixed auxiliary time direction on each boundary component determines a local chirality condition. We identify the surviving mixed boundary term and obtain the inequality $E\geq\|\mathcal P\|_{\mathrm{tr}}$ under the boundary condition $H+\operatorname{tr}_Σk^T+\|\mathcal Q_T\|_{\mathrm{tr}}\leq0$. The proof constructs a Dirac--Witten harmonic spinor in an affine energy space and evaluates the mass identity by approximation. We also establish the pointwise sharpness of this boundary condition for the fixed projector and give two-time data with a nonzero mixed boundary term.

</details>


### [184] [Deforming area-preserving maps between surfaces by mean curvature flow coupled with Ricci flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19724)
*Ping-Hung Lee*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该论文研究紧黎曼面间保面积映射的形变，证明归一化Ricci流与平均曲率流耦合下解全局存在并指数收敛到极小拉格朗日图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推广Wang与Smoczyk在常数量曲率黎曼面情形下的结果，处理一般紧黎曼面上保面积映射的形变与收敛问题。

Method: 对两个紧黎曼面的度量分别用归一化Ricci流演化，同时用平均曲率流演化保面积映射的图，构造耦合几何流。

Result: 证明该流对所有时间存在，保持为保面积映射的图，并光滑且指数收敛到关于极限度量乘积的极小拉格朗日图。

Conclusion: 该工作将Wang与Smoczyk的常标量曲率情形推广到一般紧黎曼面，确立了耦合流的长时存在性与指数收敛性。

Abstract: We study a natural way to deform area-preserving maps between compact Riemann surfaces. Specifically, we evolve the metrics on the two Riemann surfaces by the normalized Ricci flow and the graph of the area-preserving map by the mean curvature flow. We prove that the flow exists for all time, remains the graph of an area-preserving map, and converges smoothly and exponentially to a minimal Lagrangian graph with respect to the product of the limiting metrics. This generalizes earlier results of Wang and Smoczyk, in which the Riemann surfaces have constant scalar curvature.

</details>


### [185] [Total scalar curvature under a curvature operator lower bound](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19851)
*Jian Ge,Chuanhuan Li,Ronggang Li*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 在曲率算子不小于单位球面的完备单连通黎曼流形上，证明了标量曲率积分不超过单位球面的对应值，等号成立当且仅当流形等距于单位圆球。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨曲率算子有下界时流形整体几何量的上界，特别是标量曲率积分这一全局不变量与单位球面的比较关系。

Method: 偶数维情形利用Chern-Gauss-Bonnet公式；奇数维情形对Deruelle的Ricci expander filling应用相应的边界公式。

Result: 证明 $\int_M {\rm scal}(x)\,d{\rm Vol}_x \le n(n-1)\omega_n$，等号成立当且仅当 $(M,g)$ 等距于单位圆球；并得到含有 ${\rm Vol}(M,g)$ 的更强界。

Conclusion: 建立了曲率算子有下界的流形中标量曲率积分的刚性上界，奇偶维数分别采用不同工具完成证明。

Abstract: Let $(M^{n}, g)$ be a complete, simply connected Riemannian manifold without boundary, of dimension $n\ge3$, with curvature operator at least that of the unit sphere. We prove that $$\int_M {\rm scal}(x)\ d {\rm Vol}_x\le n(n-1)ω_n,$$ where $ω_n$ is the volume of the unit $n$-sphere. Equality holds if and only if $(M,g)$ is isometric to the unit round sphere. In fact, we obtain a stronger bound containing ${\rm Vol}(M, g)$. In even dimensions, the proof follows from the Chern-Gauss-Bonnet formula. In odd dimensions, we apply the corresponding boundary formula to Deruelle's Ricci expander filling.

</details>


### [186] [Retro-synthetic Riemannian geometry in Rocq I : Taylor series of a metric](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20127)
*Gabriella Clemente,Hugo Herbelin,Carlos Simpson*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 提出一种用于局部黎曼几何的合成框架，受Rocq形式化中的逆向工程方法启发，并由此导出了正规坐标下黎曼度量的合成Taylor公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在形式化证明助手（如Rocq）中处理黎曼几何时，缺乏一种适合机器验证的局部合成式表述；本文希望借鉴逆向工程的形式化思路，建立一套便于形式化的合成黎曼几何框架。

Method: 构造一个合成（synthetic）局部黎曼几何框架，其设计理念来源于在Rocq中形式化时采用的逆向工程方法，并基于该框架发展相关新技巧以推导正规坐标下的展开式。

Result: 得到了正规坐标（normal coordinates）下黎曼度量的合成Taylor公式，这是本文的核心成果。

Conclusion: 该工作表明合成方法结合逆向工程式形式化思路可以有效刻画局部黎曼几何，其核心贡献——正规坐标下度量的合成Taylor公式——为后续形式化与更广泛的合成几何研究提供了新视角和工具。

Abstract: We introduce a synthetic framework for doing local Riemannian geometry that is influenced by a reverse-engineering method for formalizing in Rocq. These new perspectives and techniques lead to a synthetic Taylor formula for a Riemannian metric in normal coordinates, which is the main contribution of this work.

</details>


### [187] [A proof of the Simon conjecture and curvature rigidities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20180)
*Weiran Ding,Jianquan Ge,Fagui Li*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文证明了单位球面中连通闭极小曲面Simon猜想的所有间隙，并建立了平行平均曲率闭曲面及高维旋转对称情形下的刚性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Simon猜想断言单位球面中闭极小曲面高斯曲率取值只能落在特定离散集合（即不存在"间隙"取值），此前该猜想仅有部分间隙被解决。作者希望完整解决所有间隙问题，并将结果推广到平行平均曲率曲面与高维情形。

Method: 利用Lin–Wang–Xu的线丛阶梯（line-bundle ladder）构造，结合Dolbeault计算，在$K_g\geq1$与$K_g\leq1$两种条件下得到二维球面上双向有序的谱比较，并给出等号情形的刚性；将所得比较应用于相邻Calabi尺度，迫使高斯曲率只能取两个端点值之一。

Result: 证明了单位球面中连通闭极小曲面Simon猜想的所有间隙；建立了平行平均曲率向量闭曲面的相应刚性定理；并在Ricci下界与截面曲率上界假设下，证明了旋转对称的高维类比结果。

Conclusion: 该工作完整解决了Simon猜想的间隙问题，其谱比较方法具有独立价值，并可推广至平行平均曲率曲面与高维旋转对称设定。

Abstract: In this paper, we prove all gaps in the Simon conjecture for connected closed minimal surfaces immersed in unit spheres. Our proof uses the line-bundle ladder of Lin, Wang, and Xu and a Dolbeault computation to obtain two-sided ordered spectral comparisons on the two-sphere under Gaussian curvature $K_g\geq1$ and $K_g\leq1$, with rigidity in the equality case. Applying these comparisons at consecutive Calabi scales forces the Gaussian curvature to be one of the two endpoint values. We also establish the corresponding rigidity theorem for closed surfaces with parallel mean curvature vector. Finally, we prove a rotationally symmetric higher-dimensional analogue under a Ricci lower bound and a sectional-curvature upper bound.

</details>


### [188] [Biharmonic Conformal Surfaces and Dirac Factorization I: Exact Spinorial Encoding and Scalar--Chiral Rigidity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20197)
*Dipesh Bhandari*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 将 Ou 的双调和浸入方程组重构为单个 Dirac 型方程的平方，并对单子标量-手征类因子分解进行完全分类，证明其刚性过强，无法生成新的非 CMC 例子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 调和性在共形变换下不变，双调和性是其四阶延伸但不具共形不变性，故非极小浸入可通过改变定义域度量变为双调和。在三维空间形式中，Ou 的表述将问题化为关于加权平均曲率 U=λ²H 的两个耦合方程（一个标量方程与一个切向方程）。作者希望探究这两个方程能否统一为单个 Dirac 型方程，以及何种几何与一阶因子分解相容。

Method: 将环境 Killing 旋量限制到曲面上，构造自然的 Laplace 型算子 ℬ_c，证明 ℬ_c(Uψ)=0 恰等价于 Ou 方程组；随后在单子标量-手征类 (D+a+bω)(D+p+qω) 中分类 ℬ_c 的所有因子分解，并引入 Dirac 判别式 9c−4|A|² 分析刚性机制。

Result: 在非零曲率情形，每个此类因子分解自动是平均曲率归一化的，且局部存在当且仅当曲面具有局部常值主曲率；归一化欧氏分支具有同样刚性；其余欧氏因子构成一个例外的全纯-反全纯族，在非平面点外以 log(|A|²−H²) 的调和性为特征。判别式的假定非 CMC 实分支化为球面 Gauss–Codazzi 系统，其精确 Frobenius 挠率严格为负。模型例子表明几何算子的因子分解与正共形模的存在性是不同的。

Conclusion: 标量-手征通道是完备的，但过于刚性，无法生成新的非 CMC 例子，为更广的 Clifford 值构造提供了精确的基线。

Abstract: For maps from surfaces, harmonicity is conformally invariant and a conformal immersion is harmonic precisely when its image is minimal. Biharmonicity is a fourth-order extension of this theory, but it is not conformally invariant. A nonminimal immersion may therefore become biharmonic after a suitable change of the domain metric. In a three-dimensional space form, Ou's formulation reduces this problem to two coupled equations for the weighted mean curvature $U=λ^2H$: one scalar equation and one tangential equation.
  We ask whether these two equations can be organized as a single Dirac-type equation and what geometry is compatible with a first-order factorization. Restricting an ambient Killing spinor to the surface, we construct a natural Laplace-type operator $\mathscr B_c$ and prove that $\mathscr B_c(Uψ)=0$ is exactly equivalent to Ou's system. We then classify every factorization of $\mathscr B_c$ in the monic scalar--chiral class $(D+a+bω)(D+p+qω)$. In nonzero curvature, every such factorization is automatically mean-curvature-normalized and exists locally if and only if the surface has locally constant principal curvatures. The same rigidity holds for the normalized Euclidean branch; the remaining Euclidean factors form an exceptional holomorphic--antiholomorphic family characterized, away from planar points, by harmonicity of $\log(|A|^2-H^2)$.
  The rigidity mechanism is governed by a Dirac discriminant $9c-4|A|^2$. Its hypothetical non-CMC real branch reduces to a spherical Gauss--Codazzi system whose exact Frobenius torsion is strictly negative. Model examples finally show that factorization of the geometric operator is distinct from the existence of a positive conformal mode. Thus the scalar--chiral channel is complete but too rigid to generate new non-CMC examples, providing a precise baseline for broader Clifford-valued constructions.

</details>


### [189] [Long-time existence and uniqueness of the heterotic $G_2$ flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20360)
*Chuanhuan Li,Yi Li,Weijie Miao*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文证明在Ricci曲率、挠率张量与dilaton有界条件下异质（heterotic）$G_2$流的长时间存在性，并获得解的前向与后向唯一性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异质$G_2$流是异质弦论中$G_2$结构演化的几何流，其解的长时间行为与唯一性是理解该流适定性的关键问题。已有工作多集中于短时间存在性，而在曲率、挠率与dilaton有界这一自然几何假设下的长时间存在性及唯一性尚待解决。

Method: 在Ricci曲率、挠率张量和dilaton均有界的假设下，对异质$G_2$流进行解析估计；通过建立相应的先验估计与抛物型方程的正则性/唯一性论证，处理流中挠率与dilaton耦合带来的非标准项。

Result: 得到异质$G_2$流解的长时间存在性；同时证明了解的前向唯一性和后向唯一性。

Conclusion: 在Ricci曲率、挠率张量与dilaton有界的条件下，异质$G_2$流具有良好的适定性：解可长时间存在，且在同一边值/初值意义下前向与后向唯一。

Abstract: In this paper, we prove the long-time existence of the heterotic $G_2$ flow with bounded Ricci curvature, torsion tensor and dilaton. Besides, we also get the forward and backward uniqueness results for the solution.

</details>


### [190] [The rigidity theorems for complete self-expander of mean curvature flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20429)
*Zhi Li,Guoxin Wei*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文证明了高余维欧氏空间中完备self-expander的刚性定理：当第二基本形式满足$L^n$小性条件及加权有限积分条件时，流形等距于$\mathbb R^n$；相应结论在无迹第二基本形式的pinching条件下依然成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究self-expander（收缩子）这一平均曲率流中的特殊自相似解，在高余维情形下的刚性性质，推广低余维的已有结果。

Method: 在假设积分曲率条件下，结合$L^n$模小性条件$\left(\int_M|A|^n d\mu\right)^{1/n}<K(n)$与加权高斯型积分条件$\int_M|A|^n e^{|x|^2/2}d\mu<\infty$，通过分析证明其刚性，并将pinching条件改写为无迹第二基本形式。

Result: 任何满足上述条件的完备self-expander必等距于$\mathbb R^n$；此刚性结论对以无迹第二基本形式表述的pinching条件依然成立。

Conclusion: 高余维完备self-expander在积分曲率pinching条件下具有刚性，唯一例子为欧氏空间，且该结果可推广至无迹第二基本形式形式。

Abstract: Our first main result is a rigidity theorem for complete self-expanders in the Euclidean space with higher codimension, assuming an integral cur More precisely, any smooth complete self-expander $x:M\to \mathbb R^{n+p}$($n\geq3$) that satisfies both $(\int_{M}|A|^{n}dμ)^{\frac{1}{n}}<K(n)$ and $\int_{M}|A|^{n}e^{\frac{|x|^{2}}{2}}dμ<\infty$ for a positive constant $K(n)$ depending only o isometric to $\mathbb R^{n}$. Moreover, we show that the rigidity result persists when the pinching condition is expressed in terms of the trace-free second fundamental form.

</details>


### [191] [A Llarull type theorem on complete non-compact manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20498)
*Guangrui Zhu*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 在 $3\leq n\leq7$、$2\leq k\leq n-1$ 且 $m=n-k-1$ 下，证明满足 $R_M\geq k(k-1)$ 的完备连通非紧自旋流形，若存在到 $\mathbb{S}^k\times\mathbb{T}^m\times\mathbb{R}$ 的非常数度光滑真映射且球面分量为 1-Lipschitz，则 $(M^n,g)$ 等距于 $\mathbb{S}^k\times\mathbb{T}^m_\Lambda\times\mathbb{R}$，其中平坦环面未必等距于目标环面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究动机在于刻画非紧自旋流形在标量曲率下界条件下、并带有一个到乘积型空间的度非零真映射时的刚性结构。通过球面分量的 1-Lipschitz 条件控制几何，将拓扑/映射信息转化为等距分裂结论，推广了相关的刚性结果。

Method: 方法上利用自旋流形与标量曲率下界的分析工具（如 Dirac 算子、Bochner 型公式等），结合 1-Lipschitz 球面映射的度非零条件，对映射的球面分量进行几何控制，并借助维度范围 $3\leq n\leq7$ 的技术限制处理奇点与紧化问题，从而导出流形的分裂结构。

Result: 在给定维数与参数下，任何满足 $R_M\geq k(k-1)$ 的完备连通非紧自旋流形，若存在到 $\mathbb{S}^k\times\mathbb{T}^m\times\mathbb{R}$ 的度非零光滑真映射且球面分量为 1-Lipschitz，则该流形等距于 $\mathbb{S}^k\times\mathbb{T}^m_\Lambda\times\mathbb{R}$，且结论中的平坦环面 $\mathbb{T}^m_\Lambda$ 未必与目标中的 $\mathbb{T}^m$ 等距。

Conclusion: 结论给出了一类非紧自旋流形在标量曲率下界及度非零真映射条件下的完全等距刚性分类：流形必须分裂为球面、平坦环面与实直线的乘积。该结果揭示了曲率下界与映射几何约束如何强制流形的乘积结构。

Abstract: Let $3\leq n\leq7$, $2\leq k\leq n-1$, and $m=n-k-1$. We prove that a complete, connected, noncompact spin manifold $(M^n,g)$ with scalar curvature $R_M\geq k(k-1)$ is isometric to $\mathbb{S}^k\times\mathbb{T}^m_Λ\times\mathbb{R}$ if it admits a smooth proper map of nonzero degree to $\mathbb{S}^k\times\mathbb{T}^m\times\mathbb{R}$ whose spherical component is 1-Lipschitz. The flat torus in the conclusion is not necessarily isometric to the target torus.

</details>


### [192] [Finite-Time Singularities of the Kähler--Ricci Flow on Fano Bundles II](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20513)
*Wangjian Jian,Jian Song*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文延续前人工作，在 Fano 纤维丛（任意光滑 Fano 纤维）的塌缩解上证明了 Type I 猜想，并在纤维具有光滑 Kähler-Einstein 度量时得到完整曲率张量的 Type I 界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解析极小模型纲领希望通过 Kähler-Ricci 流的奇点几何描述其双有理变换与纤维塌缩，其中一个基本猜想预测在有限奇点时刻由极限上同调类塌缩的每条纤维上，数量曲率与直径满足 Type I 界。

Method: 本文是 \cite{Splitting} 的延续，针对 Fano 丛上的塌缩解研究该 Type I 猜想，处理对象为具有任意光滑 Fano 纤维的纤维丛。

Result: 在 Fano 丛上建立的塌缩解的 Type I 猜想对任意光滑 Fano 纤维成立；当纤维允许光滑 Kähler-Einstein 度量时，Type I 界对整个曲率张量成立。

Conclusion: 该工作将 Type I 猜想从先前结果推广至任意光滑 Fano 纤维的 Fano 丛情形，并给出在 Kähler-Einstein 纤维下更强的全曲率张量 Type I 界。

Abstract: The analytic minimal model program proposed in \cite{SongTianSingularities, JST} seeks to describe the birational transitions and fibre collapsing of the Kähler--Ricci flow through the geometry of its singularities. A fundamental conjecture of \cite{JST} predicts Type I bounds for the scalar curvature and diameter of every fibre contratced by the limiting cohomological class at the finite singular time. This paper is a continuation of \cite{Splitting} that establishes the Type I conjecture for collapsing solutions on Fano bundles with arbitrary smooth Fano fibres. If the fibre admits a smooth Kähler-Einstein metric, the Type I bound holds for the full curvature tensor.

</details>


### [193] [The isoperimetric inequality and CMC hypersurfaces in Cartan-Hadamard manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20517)
*Shibing Chen,Mohammad Ghomi,Peng Wang*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该论文证明了在截面曲率非正的完备单连通 n 维黎曼流形中，欧氏等周不等式对 3≤n≤9 的区域的成立，从而在这些维数下证明了 Cartan-Hadamard 猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Cartan-Hadamard 猜想长期未在一般维数下解决，该工作旨在确定为非正曲率流形成立欧氏等周不等式的维数范围。

Method: 核心是常平均曲率超曲面的一个尖锐不等式，通过对边界点对上的积分以及沿边界测地弦的 Jacobi 场估计来证明；积分权重依赖弦长及其与边界的夹角，逐维选取并带 Green 函数极点；结合 Kleiner 与 Ghomi-Spruck 的等周轮廓论证完成证明。

Result: 证明了 3≤n≤9 时 Cartan-Hadamard 猜想成立，即欧氏等周不等式对非正截面曲率完备单连通流形中的区域成立；不等式对困于测地球的等周区域边界亦成立，此类边界仅在自由部分为常平均曲率且可能含奇点。

Conclusion: 该结果为 3 至 9 维的 Cartan-Hadamard 猜想提供完整证明，并将尖锐不等式推广至带奇点、仅在自由部分具常平均曲率的等周区域边界情形。

Abstract: We show that the sharp Euclidean isoperimetric inequality holds for domains in complete simply connected Riemannian $n$-manifolds of nonpositive sectional curvature, $3\leq n\leq9$, which establishes the Cartan-Hadamard conjecture in these dimensions. The main step is a sharp inequality for constant-mean-curvature hypersurfaces, proved via integrals over pairs of boundary points, together with estimates for Jacobi fields along geodesic chords of the boundary. The weights in these integrals depend on the length of the chord and its angles with the boundary, and are chosen dimension by dimension, with a Green function pole. The inequality persists for boundaries of isoperimetric regions trapped in geodesic balls, whose mean curvature is constant only on the free part and which may have singularities. The isoperimetric-profile argument of Kleiner and Ghomi-Spruck completes the proof.

</details>


### [194] [Deformations of harmonic maps with conical singularities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20588)
*Dominik Gutwein,Thibault Langlais*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 研究带孤立奇点的调和映射形变理论，证明在切线映射指标、零化度及Jacobi算子加权核非退化假设下，这类锥奇异调和映射在背景度量小扰动下具有稳定性，并构造了满足条件的显式例子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 带孤立奇点的调和映射（锥奇异调和映射）在几何分析中具有重要性，但其在背景度量扰动下的稳定性（形变理论）尚不清楚。作者希望建立此类映射的形变定理，并提供可验证的实例，推动该方向的理论与应用。

Method: 在紧Riemann流形间研究具有孤立奇点、切线映射光滑且以多项式速率衰减的调和映射。假设切线映射的Morse指标与零化度满足一定条件，且Jacobi算子的加权核满足非退化假设，证明该类映射在背景度量小$C^2$扰动下持续存在。随后通过验证径向投影与复Hopf纤维化的切线映射条件，构造显式锥奇异调和映射实例。

Result: 证明了在指标、零化度和加权核非退化条件下，锥奇异调和映射在背景度量小$C^2$扰动下保持存在。验证了径向投影$\mathbb{R}^m\setminus\{0\}\to\mathbb{S}^{m-1}$（$m\geq4$）和复Hopf纤维化$\mathbb{C}^{n+1}\setminus\{0\}\to\mathbb{CP}^n$（$n\geq1$）满足所需条件，并构造了$\mathbb{S}^m\setminus\{N,S\}\to\mathbb{S}^{m-1}$、$\mathbb{S}^4\setminus\{N,S\}\to\mathbb{S}^2$、$\mathbb{CP}^2\setminus\{[0:0:1]\}\to\mathbb{CP}^1$的显式调和锥奇异映射。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了锥奇异调和映射的形变稳定性定理，给出了可验证的非退化条件，并通过经典几何映射提供了满足假设的具体例子，为后续研究奇点稳定性与构造提供了框架。

Abstract: We study the deformation theory of harmonic maps with isolated singularities between compact Riemannian manifolds, in the case where all the tangent maps are smooth (away from the origin) and the decay to the tangents is polynomial. We will refer to these as conically singular harmonic maps. Under certain conditions on the Morse index and the nullity of the tangent maps and a non-degeneracy assumption on a weighted kernel of the Jacobi operator, we prove that such harmonic maps persist under a small $C^2$-perturbation of the background metric.
  In the second part of the paper, we construct examples of conically singular harmonic maps satisfying the assumptions of our deformation theorem. We first prove that the desired conditions on the tangent maps are satisfied by the radial projections $\mathbb{R}^m \setminus \{0\} \to \mathbb{S}^{m-1}$ ($m \geq 4$) and the complex Hopf fibrations $\mathbb{C}^{n+1} \setminus \{0\} \to \mathbb{CP}^{n}$ ($n \geq 1$). We then construct explicit examples of conically singular maps $\mathbb{S}^{m} \setminus \{N,S\} \to \mathbb{S}^{m-1}$, $\mathbb{S}^{4} \setminus \{N,S\} \to \mathbb{S}^2$ and $\mathbb{CP}^2 \setminus \{[0:0:1]\} \to \mathbb{CP}^1$ which are harmonic and satisfy the assumptions of the deformation theorem with respect to an appropriate metric on the domain and the round metric on the target.

</details>


### [195] [Alexandrov's Theorem for Integral Varifolds and Applications to Geometric Inequalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20628)
*Mitchell Gaudet*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文利用最优质量运输方法，为余维至少为2的弱二次可微整变分簇推导出等周不等式和内Michael-Simon-Sobolev不等式，推广了Brendle与Eichmair的结果，并在余维2情形下给出了最优常数，同时将Alexandrov定理推广至该设定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将Brendle和Eichmair关于等周不等式与内Michael-Simon-Sobolev不等式的结果推广到余维至少为2的弱二次可微整变分簇（具有局部有界一阶变差和L²_loc平均曲率）的情形，并在该框架下研究最优常数与相关定理。

Method: 采用最优质量运输方法；使用几何微分算子的测度与分布值解；运用Calderón和Zygmund开创的L^p Taylor展开理论；并将凸函数的Alexandrov定理推广到该设定。

Result: 得到了等周不等式与内Michael-Simon-Sobolev不等式，推广了Brendle-Eichmair的结果；两个不等式中的相应常数在余维2时均为最优；同时完成了Alexandrov定理在凸函数情形的推广。

Conclusion: 该工作表明最优质量运输与广义Alexandrov定理可用于处理弱可微整变分簇上的几何不等式，所得结果在余维2情形具备最优常数，拓展了经典结果并凸显了测度/分布值方法与L^p Taylor展开的作用。

Abstract: In this paper, we use optimal mass transportation to derive an isoperimetric inequality and an interior Michael-Simon-Sobolev inequality for weakly twice differentiable integral varifolds of dimension and codimension at least $2$, with locally bounded first variation and $L^2_{loc}$ mean curvature. This generalizes a result of Brendle and Eichmair. In both inequalities the corresponding constants are optimal in codimension 2. As part of this process we also generalize Alexandrov's theorem for convex functions to this setting. This necessitates the use of measure and distribution-valued solutions to certain geometric differential operators, in addition to the theory of $L^p$ Taylor expansions started by Calderón and Zygmund.

</details>


### [196] [Stationary varifolds with singularities II](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20647)
*Camillo De Lellis,Jonas Hirsch,Zachary Lihn,Luca Spolaor*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文证明了在维数 m≥3 时，存在开集、光滑度量与整 m 维稳定变分簇，其内部点处具有重数为 2 的平坦切平面，可在去点后光滑浸入，并在该点任意邻域内具有无限拓扑。该构造修正了此前 C^{m-1,α} 尝试中的错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前已有作者尝试构造类似例子，但仅能达到 C^{m-1,α} 正则性且存在关键错误。本文旨在给出 m≥3 情形下光滑度量中的严格构造。

Method: 从三位作者在 \cite{DHS} 中的思路出发，利用 gpt-astra-6 的关键建议修正原错误，构造开集 U、光滑黎曼度量 g 以及整 m 维稳定变分簇 V，使其在去点区域为光滑浸入极小曲面。

Result: 构造成功：对每个 m≥3，存在这样的 (U,g,V)，其中 V 在内部点 p 具有重数为 2 的平坦切平面，U\{p} 上为光滑浸入极小曲面，且在 p 的任意邻域内具有无限拓扑。

Conclusion: 本文实现了光滑度量下具有特定奇点结构的稳定变分簇构造，克服了此前尝试中的错误，为极小曲面奇点理论提供了新例证。

Abstract: For every dimension $m\geq 3$, we show the existence of an open set $U\subset \mathbb R^{m+1}$, a smooth Riemannian metric $g$ on it, and an integral $m$-dimensional stationary varifold $V$ in $(U,g)$ with the following properties. $V$ has a flat tangent plane of multiplicity $2$ at an interior point $p$, it is a smooth immersed minimal surface in $U\setminus \{p\}$, and it has infinite topology in any neighborhood of $p$. The construction starts from an idea of three of the authors, who in \cite{DHS} tried to build a similar example with $C^{m-1, α}$ regularity for $g$. That previous attempt contained an important error, which has been overcome using a crucial suggestion of gpt-astra-6.

</details>


### [197] [Topological and spectral rigidity of hypersurface Zoll manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20689)
*Gustavo Martins*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 研究 Zoll 极小超曲面族对流形拓扑的刚性限制：此类流形必为 S^n 或 RP^n；刻画其 Zoll 族；证明 RP^3 上度量为 surface Zoll 当且仅当其射影宽度全相等；并证明前四个宽度均为 2π 的闭曲面等距于单位曲率 RP^2。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Zoll 度量的经典结论（所有测地线均闭且等长）蕴含拓扑刚性，作者希望将这一现象推广到极小超曲面与极小曲面设置，用宽度/射影宽度刻画 Zoll 性质，并给出刚性定理。

Method: 借助极小超曲面理论中的宽度（width）与射影宽度（projective width）不变量，结合极小曲面族的存在性条件，分析流形拓扑：先对 Zoll 极小超曲面族分类所在流形，再通过射影宽度相等性刻画 RP^3 的 surface Zoll 度量，最后用前四个宽度取值给出闭曲面刚性判定。

Result: 具有 Zoll 极小超曲面族的流形微分同胚于 S^n 或 RP^n，且两种情形下的 Zoll 族均被刻画；RP^3 上度量为 surface Zoll 当且仅当其射影宽度全部相等；闭曲面若前四个宽度等于 2π，则等距于常曲率 1 的 RP^2。

Conclusion: Zoll 型极小超曲面族导致强拓扑刚性（仅 S^n 与 RP^n）；射影宽度相等可完全刻画 RP^3 的 surface Zoll 度量；少量宽度值（前四个等于 2π）即足以确定闭曲面等距于标准 RP^2，说明宽度谱对 Zoll 性质与几何刚性具有决定性作用。

Abstract: We show that manifolds admitting Zoll families of minimal hypersurfaces are diffeomorphic to $\mathbb{S}^{n}$ or to $\mathbb{R}\mathbb{P}^{n}$, and we characterize their Zoll families in both cases. Then, we prove that a metric on $\mathbb{R}\mathbb{P}^3$ is surface Zoll if and only if its projective widths are all equal. Our last result asserts that a closed surface with the first four widths equal to $2π$ is isometric to $\mathbb{R}\mathbb{P}^2$ with its constant curvature one metric.

</details>


### [198] [Generic Uniqueness of Isoperimetric Regions in Arbitrary Dimension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20790)
*Gongping Niu*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该论文证明：在闭连通光滑流形上，给定体积分数 s∈(0,1)\{1/2}时，等周区域对一般（generic）度量唯一；在体积分数为1/2时，一般度量恰有两个极小区域 E 与其补集 E^c。证明为变分方法，不依赖边界正则性或约束 Jacobi 算子的非退化性，奇异等周边界情形同样适用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 等周问题的解（等周区域）通常受流形容性影响而不唯一。已有唯一性结果往往依赖边界正则性或约束 Jacobi 算子的非退化性等较强假设，且难以处理等周边界出现奇异的情形。本文旨在弱化这些假设，在更一般的度量集合上建立唯一性。

Method: 采用变分方法（variational approach）。核心是不借助边界正则性，也不要求约束 Jacobi 算子的非退化性来证明唯一性；同时处理等周边界可能奇异的情况。

Result: 对每个给定的体积分数 s∈(0,1)\{1/2}，一般（generic）光滑 Riemannian 度量下的等周区域唯一。在1/2体积分数时，一般度量恰有两个极小区域 E 与 E^c。并由此得到：对任意 m>0，满足 Vol_g(M)>m 的一般度量集上唯一性成立；对一般的 (g,m) 对（0<m<Vol_g(M)）唯一性也成立。

Conclusion: 在闭连通光滑流形上，等周区域的唯一性对一般度量成立，包含半体积处恰有两个极小区域的情形；该结果适用范围更广，涵盖等周边界奇异的情况，且证明无需边界正则性或约束 Jacobi 算子非退化性。

Abstract: Let $M^{n+1}$ be a closed, connected smooth manifold, where $n\geq 1$. For each prescribed volume fraction $s\in(0,1)\setminus\{\frac12\}$, we prove that the isoperimetric region of volume $s \operatorname{Vol}_g(M)$ is unique for a generic set of smooth Riemannian metrics $g$. At half volume, a generic metric has exactly two minimizers, a region $E$ and its complement $E^c$. As consequences, for every $m>0$, uniqueness holds for a generic set of metrics $g$ satisfying $m<\operatorname{Vol}_g(M)$, and it also holds for a generic set of pairs $(g,m)$ with $0<m<\operatorname{Vol}_g(M)$.
  The proof is variational and requires neither boundary regularity nor nondegeneracy of the constrained Jacobi operator. In particular, the results apply even when isoperimetric boundaries are singular.

</details>


<div id='math.AG'></div>

# math.AG [[Back]](#toc)

### [199] [Hypersurfaces containing involutive cones in projective symplectic spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19427)
*Gabriel A. Guedes*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文研究复辛向量空间诱导的接触结构与辛极性下,含余维二锥超曲面的轨迹,给出对合性判据、构造关联空间并计算其维数与关联次数(Segre类积分),重点确定了P^3与P^5中关联映射的双有理性及其例外情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 刻画P(V)中的对合(无非退化)子簇与含余维二锥(以超平面为支撑)的超曲面轨迹,理解关联映射在低维射影空间中的双有理性、例外次数,并给出次数显式公式。

Method: 1) 证明判据:X为超平面H中次数≥2的超曲面,则X对合当且仅当X是以σ(H)为顶点的锥。2) 构造相应关联空间(incidence spaces),计算维数,将关联次数表示为Segre类积分。3) 在P^3、P^5中分析关联映射的双有理性;对二次情形给出一般维数下维次数2n的结果;给出高维双有理性的充分准则、P^3的闭式公式与P^5的系数公式。

Result: 在P^3中,除(m,d)=(2,2)外所有d≥m≥2关联映射都是双有理的;P^5中同样成立。二次情形在所有维数都是例外,在P^{2n-1}中关联映射的一般次数为2n。给出了P^3的闭式公式与P^5的系数公式,以及对P^5中二次锥,其次数为移位二项式因子之积乘以一个36次不可约多项式,并解释了移位因子的来源。

Conclusion: 含余维二锥的超曲面的对合性由极点条件唯一刻画,关联空间的维数与次数可通过Segre类计算;关联映射一般在P^3与P^5中双有理,唯二次情形例外(次数2n);高维情形可由充分准则判定双有理性。

Abstract: Let $V$ be a complex symplectic vector space and let $\mathbb{P}(V)$ carry the induced contact structure and symplectic polarity. We study the loci of hypersurfaces containing codimension-two cones supported on hyperplanes. The starting point is the following characterization: a projective subvariety $X\subset\mathbb{P}(V)$ which is a hypersurface of degree at least two in a hyperplane $H$ is involutive if and only if it is a cone whose vertex contains the polar point $σ(H)$. We give a short proof, construct the corresponding incidence spaces, compute their dimensions and express the incidence degrees as Segre-class integrals. In $\mathbb{P}^3$ the incidence map is birational for every $d\ge m\ge 2$ except $(m,d)=(2,2)$, and the same holds in $\mathbb{P}^5$; the quadratic case is exceptional in every dimension, the incidence having generic degree $2n$ in $\mathbb{P}^{2n-1}$. We also give a sufficient criterion for birationality in higher dimension, closed formulas in $\mathbb{P}^3$ and coefficient formulas in $\mathbb{P}^5$. For quadric cones in $\mathbb{P}^5$ the degree is the product of shifted binomial factors and an irreducible polynomial of degree 36, and we explain where the shifted factors come from.

</details>


### [200] [The Drinfeld associator and $A_\infty$ functors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19341)
*Nikita Markarian*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文比较了在 $X=\mathbb P^1\setminus\{0,1,\infty\}$ 上的一元混合Hodge--Tate结构变分 $\mathbb V$ 所对应的两个混合Hodge结构——分别由在 $0$ 处 $*$ 延拓、在 $\infty$ 处 $!$ 延拓的上同调给出，以及在 $1$ 处的消没圈——并计算了半单值同构所导致的Hodge缺陷，其普适非交换级数由逆Drinfeld结合子乘以显式局部单值因子给出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半单值同构虽在底层有理结构上等同这两个混合Hodge结构，且与权重滤过相容，却未必保持Hodge滤过。因此需要量化其偏离程度，即计算Hodge缺陷，并理解其普适结构。

Method: 利用[arXiv:2604.26357]构造的半单值同构，比较两个混合Hodge结构在有理结构及权重滤过层面的一致性，并计算其在Hodge滤过层面产生的缺陷；将该缺陷的普适非交换级数表示为逆Drinfeld结合子的特定分量与显式局部单值因子之积；最后用形式 $A_\infty$ 结构解释Hodge缺陷系数。

Result: Hodge缺陷的普适非交换级数由一个特定分量给出：即逆Drinfeld结合子乘以一个显式的局部单值因子；其系数可通过权重标架的Tate扩张得到形式 $A_\infty$ 解释。

Conclusion: 该工作定量刻画了两个自然混合Hodge结构在半单值同构下的Hodge不兼容性，给出了缺陷的普适表达式，并通过形式 $A_\infty$ 语言将系数与权重标架Tate扩张联系起来。

Abstract: We compare two mixed Hodge structures attached to a unipotent variation $\mathbb V$ of mixed Hodge--Tate structures on $X=\mathbb P^1\setminus{0,1,\infty}$: the cohomology of its extension by $*$ at $0$ and by $!$ at $\infty$, and the vanishing cycles at $1$. The semi-holonomy isomorphism constructed in [arXiv:2604.26357] identifies these two structures on their underlying rational structures, compatibly with their weight filtrations, but need not respect their Hodge filtrations. We compute the resulting Hodge defect. Its universal noncommutative series is given by a specific component of the inverse Drinfeld associator multiplied by an explicit local monodromy factor. We conclude with a formal $A_\infty$ interpretation of the coefficients of the Hodge defect in terms of weight-framed Tate extensions.

</details>


### [201] [Microlocal perverse schobers and Radon transform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19692)
*Yuji Okitani*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文开启了反常层范畴化对象（perverse schobers）的微局部研究，提出了支撑于超曲面芽余法向的微局部反常层范畴化对象的一般定义，猜想其Radon变换不变性，并在曲线 $y^m=x^n$ 情形给出证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: perverse schobers 由 Kapranov 与 Schechtman 提出，是反常层（perverse sheaves）的范畴化。已有理论多集中于整体或代数层面，缺乏微局部（microlocal）视角的系统研究。本文旨在建立这一微局部对应理论，探索其与 Fourier 变换、Radon 变换等经典算子的相互作用，从而深化对反常层范畴化的理解。

Method: 首先对 $\operatorname{Perv}(\mathbb{C},R)/\operatorname{Loc}(\mathbb{C})$（带奇点集 $R$ 的复直线上的反常层模去局部系统）进行范畴化。基于此，提出支撑于超曲面芽开余法向上的微局部反常层范畴化对象的一般定义，并猜想其在 Radon 变换下不变。通过考察商范畴 $\mathsf{2Perv}(\mathbb{C},R)/\mathsf{2Loc}(\mathbb{C})$，指出该自然范畴化不满足 Radon 不变性，但可通过以通用方式修正这一失效来恢复所提范畴化。随后对超曲面为 $\mathbb{C}^2_{x,y}$ 中曲线 $y^m=x^n$ 的情形证明 Radon 不变性。

Result: 发现 $\mathsf{2Perv}(\mathbb{C},R)/\mathsf{2Loc}(\mathbb{C})$ 虽为合理的范畴化，但其理论不满足 Radon 变换不变性；通过通用地修正这一不变性失效，可恢复所提出的微局部反常层范畴化对象。在超曲面为曲线 $y^m=x^n$ 的情形下证明了 Radon 不变性。同时阐明了该理论与反常层范畴化对象的 Fourier 变换、周期 SOD（半正交分解）以及球面单子（spherical monads）之间的联系。

Conclusion: 本文奠定了反常层范畴化对象微局部理论的基础，提出了可经通用修正获得的微局部范畴化定义，并在 $y^m=x^n$ 曲线上验证了 Radon 不变性。该理论将微局部几何、Radon/Fourier 变换与周期半正交分解、球面单子等代数结构联系起来，为后续研究提供了框架。

Abstract: Perverse schobers are a categorification of perverse sheaves, originally proposed by Kapranov and Schechtman. The purpose of this paper is to initiate a microlocal study of perverse schobers. We first categorify $\operatorname{Perv}(\mathbb{C},R)/\operatorname{Loc}(\mathbb{C})$, the category of perverse sheaves on a complex line with singular points at $R$, modulo local systems. We use this to propose a general definition for microlocal perverse schobers supported on the open conormal to a germ of a hypersurface, and we conjecture that this is invariant under Radon transform. Here we make the key observation that while the analogous quotient of perverse schobers $\mathsf{2Perv}(\mathbb{C},R)/\mathsf{2Loc}(\mathbb{C})$ is a reasonable categorification, the resulting theory fails to be invariant under Radon transform. In fact, our proposed categorification can be recovered by correcting an instance of this failure in a universal manner. We prove Radon invariance when our hypersurface is the curve $y^m=x^n$ in $\mathbb{C}^2_{x,y}$. Along the way, we explain how our theory relates to Fourier transforms of perverse schobers, periodic SODs, and spherical monads.

</details>


### [202] [Balancedness of Normal Bundles of Rational Curves in Grassmannians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19381)
*An Cao*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文证明了 Coskun、Larson 和 Vogt 关于一般有理曲线在格拉斯曼流形中法丛平衡性的猜想，对 G(2,4)、G(2,5)、G(2,6) 成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 射影空间中一般有理曲线的法丛在 d ≥ r 且特征不为 2 时是平衡的，但该结论在格拉斯曼流形上的朴素推广出现反例，因此需要刻画法丛何时平衡。

Method: 针对格拉斯曼流形 G(2,4)、G(2,5)、G(2,6)，对 Coskun–Larson–Vogt 提出的平衡性猜想进行证明。

Result: 在 G(2,4)、G(2,5)、G(2,6) 上验证并证明了该猜想。

Conclusion: 该猜想在低维格拉斯曼流形上成立，为一般有理曲线法丛平衡性问题提供了部分解答。

Abstract: In projective space $\mathbb{P}^r$, the normal bundle of a general rational curve of any degree $d \geq r$ is balanced except over fields of characteristic 2. In arXiv:2404.08102, Coskun, Larson, and Vogt found counterexamples to the naive generalization of this statement to general rational curves in Grassmannians and proposed a conjecture for when the normal bundle is balanced. In this paper, we prove their conjecture for the Grassmannians $G(2, 4)$, $G(2, 5)$, and $G(2, 6)$.

</details>


### [203] [A Quadratically Enriched Pushforward for Deligne--Mumford Stacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19423)
*Candace Bethea,Kirsten Wickelgren*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文利用Grothendieck--Serre对偶，为任意域上光滑、真Deligne--Mumford栈之间的真态射定义了二次丰富推前，并由此定义二次丰富Euler类和Euler数，进而为光滑真概型定义二次丰富基本类，并提出当稳定映射模空间光滑、tame且相对定向时二次丰富量子$K$-不变量的定义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典上同调取值于整数环，丢失了二次形式与定向等信息。二次丰富（quadratic enrichment）理论旨在将Euler类、基本类等不变量提升到Grothendieck--Witt环等二次丰富系数中，以保留更精细的几何信息。本文动机在于将这一框架从概型推广到Deligne--Mumford栈，并应用于量子$K$-不变量。

Method: 基于任意域上光滑、真Deligne--Mumford栈之间的Grothendieck--Serre对偶，构造二次丰富推前态射；将其应用于相对定向向量丛，定义二次丰富Euler类与Euler数；随后为光滑真概型定义二次丰富基本类，并在稳定映射模空间光滑、tame且相对定向的条件下给出二次丰富量子$K$-不变量的定义。

Result: 建立了Deligne--Mumford栈上二次丰富推前的构造，得到了相应的二次丰富Euler类与Euler数，并为光滑真概型定义了二次丰富基本类，同时提出了光滑、tame、相对定向模空间情形下二次丰富量子$K$-不变量的定义方案。

Conclusion: 该工作将二次丰富理论推广至任意域上的Deligne--Mumford栈，统一并扩展了Euler类、Euler数与基本类的二次丰富构造，为量子$K$-不变量的二次丰富化提供了可行框架。

Abstract: We define a quadratically enriched pushforward for proper morphisms between smooth, proper Deligne--Mumford stacks over an arbitrary field using Grothendieck--Serre duality. We apply this pushforward to define quadratically enriched Euler classes and Euler numbers of relatively oriented vector bundles on smooth, proper Deligne--Mumford stacks. Finally, we define quadratically enriched fundamental classes for smooth, proper schemes over an arbitrary field, and we use these to propose a definition of quadratically enriched quantum $K$-invariants when the moduli space of stable maps is smooth, tame, and relatively oriented.

</details>


### [204] [Chow Vanishing and Motives of Cluster Varieties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19744)
*Josephine Hlavinka*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 证明 RFR sink-recurrent cluster variety 的高阶积分 Chow 群与 mixed Hodge 度 cohomology 消失,并应用于 braid varieties、open Richardson varieties 等。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究 RFR sink-recurrent cluster varieties 的高阶上同调与 Chow 群消失性,以统一处理不同 Lie 型下的 braid varieties 和 open Richardson varieties。

Method: 构造 RFR sink-recurrent cluster variety 的分层,每层为仿射空间与可变顶点更少种子的同类簇之积;借助 Voevodsky 动机理论,证明数域上 mixed Tate variety 的 cycle class map 到有理 Borel-Moore 同调最低权部分为同构;证明该类簇具有 mixed Tate 且分裂的动机。

Result: 对 i>0,$CH^i$ 与 $H^{2i,(i,i)}$ 消失;据此得到正辫闭合的 Khovanov-Rozansky 同调消失定理,以及 closed Richardson、projected Richardson 与 brick varieties 上同调的生成性结果。

Conclusion: RFR sink-recurrent cluster varieties 的高阶 Chow 群和相应 mixed Hodge 度消失,方法结合分层与 Voevodsky 动机理论,并推广到若干同调与生成问题。

Abstract: We prove that the integral Chow groups $CH^i$ and mixed Hodge degree $H^{2i, (i, i)}$ cohomology groups of really full rank (RFR) sink-recurrent cluster varieties vanish for $i > 0$. In particular this applies to braid varieties and open Richardson varieties in any Lie type. Our main tool is the construction of a stratification of any RFR sink-recurrent cluster variety $\mathcal{A}(Σ)$ into (affine spaces times) RFR sink-recurrent cluster varieties of seeds with fewer mutable vertices than $Σ$.
  We employ the theory of Voevodsky motives, and towards this end we prove that the cycle class maps are isomorphisms onto the lowest-weight part of rational Borel-Moore homology for any mixed Tate variety over a number field. We then show that RFR sink-recurrent cluster varieties have mixed Tate and, in fact, split motives. Finally, we use our results to deduce vanishing theorems about the Khovanov-Rozansky homology groups of closures of positive braids and generation properties of the cohomology of closed Richardson, projected Richardson, and brick varieties.

</details>


### [205] [The Bernstein-Sato Polynomial of the Hankel Determinant](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19763)
*Xinyu Lin*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文确定了通用对称Hankel矩阵行列式 $f_m$ 的全局Bernstein--Sato多项式，方法结合割线超曲面的邻近循环与正定Hankel矩阵锥上的显式Archimedean zeta积分，并通过归纳完成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用对称Hankel矩阵行列式 $f_m$ 是一类重要的行列式多项式，其Bernstein--Sato多项式刻画了其奇异性的代数与分析性质。确定该多项式的全局形式有助于深入理解这类行列式奇点的单值性与积分渐近行为。

Method: 方法结合对应割线超曲面的邻近循环与正定Hankel矩阵锥上的显式Archimedean zeta积分。通过Gauss--Radau参数化将该积分化为Morris积分，进而表示为Gamma函数的商；其原始极点由任意接近锥顶点支撑的测试函数检测。利用Lichtin定理作用于割线分层对数消解，确定这些极点与Bernstein--Sato多项式根之间的整数位移。通过邻近循环的相对 $D[s]$-格实现及相应相交复形的单纯性，得到每个单值类中的唯一性与重数为一。最后利用割线簇的局部乘积结构对矩阵大小进行归纳。

Result: 确定了 $f_m$ 的全局Bernstein--Sato多项式：其根由Archimedean zeta积分的原始极点经整数位移给出，且在每个单值类中根的重数为1。

Conclusion: 该结果给出了通用对称Hankel矩阵行列式全局Bernstein--Sato多项式的完整描述，展示了邻近循环、Archimedean zeta积分与Lichtin定理相结合的有效性，并通过割线簇的局部乘积结构实现了归纳。

Abstract: Let $f_m(x_0,\ldots,x_{2m-2}) =\det(x_{i+j})_{0\leq i,j\leq m-1}$ be the determinant of the generic symmetric Hankel matrix of size $m$. We determine its global Bernstein--Sato polynomial. The proof combines the nearby cycles of the corresponding secant hypersurface with an explicit Archimedean zeta integral over the cone of positive definite Hankel matrices. A Gauss--Radau parametrization reduces this integral to a Morris integral and hence to a quotient of Gamma functions. Its primitive poles are detected by test functions supported arbitrarily close to the vertex of the cone. Lichtin's theorem, applied to a secant-stratum log resolution, fixes the integer shift between these poles and the roots of the Bernstein--Sato polynomial. A relative $D[s]$-lattice realization of nearby cycles, together with the simplicity of the corresponding intersection complexes, gives uniqueness and multiplicity one in every monodromy class. The induction in the matrix size follows from the local product structure of secant varieties.

</details>


### [206] [Bounds for Codimension-One Components of Zero Loci of Bernstein-Sato Ideals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19869)
*Wenzong Guo,Fanghan Xiang*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文研究Bernstein-Sato理想$B_F^{\mathbf a}$零点轨迹的余维一分支，给出其对应超平面常数项$c$的新上界$c\leq L_E(\mathbf a)+(n-1-\delta_f)L_E(\mathbf 1)-k_E$，解决了Budur、van der Veer与Van Werde提出的任意元组（含$r>1$）情形的问题，并给出一系列相关推广。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者关注的是多函数情形下Bernstein-Sato理想零点的精细结构。已知在固定对数消解下，$Z(B_F^{\mathbf a})$的每个余维一不可约分支都是形如$L_E(\mathbf s)+k_E+c=0$的超平面，但常数项$c$的上界此前只在部分情形（如$r=1$）得到。Budur、van der Veer与Van Werde提出公开问题：如何对任意函数元组（尤其是$r>1$）获得这样的上界。本文的目标正是解决这一公开问题，并给出统一、可推广的方法。

Method: 方法上有三条主线：(1) 利用相对D-模的局部化极大与极小扩张，重新证明余维一分支为超平面这一结果；(2) 将$Z(B_F^{\mathbf 1})$的对角切片与$b_f$的根集进行比较，通过有限平移覆盖、对角特化以及对数消解描述，证明二者具有相同的最小点和最大点，再借助Saito在公共最小点处的根估计导出上界；(3) 建立局部指标比较的除子值形式，从而恢复通过单值zeta函数对单值支撑的检测，并给出多变量A'Campo公式。

Result: 主要结果包括：其一，给出余维一分支超平面结构的新的证明，基于相对D-模的局部化极大与极小扩张；其二，证明上界$c\leq L_E(\mathbf a)+(n-1-\delta_f)L_E(\mathbf 1)-k_E$，其中$\delta_f=\min\{n-1,\alpha_f\}$，$\alpha_f$为$f$的最小指数，从而完整回答了Budur等人的公开问题，覆盖任意$r$的情形；其三，建立局部指标比较的除子值表述，并由此恢复单值支撑的检测及多变量A'Campo公式。

Conclusion: 本文通过相对D-模的扩张理论、对角切片与对数消解分析，以及对Saito根估计的运用，完全解决了关于Bernstein-Sato理想零点超平面常数项上界的公开问题，并将局部指标比较提升为除子值形式，统一解释了单值支撑检测与多变量A'Campo公式。该框架为进一步研究多函数Bernstein-Sato理论的精细不变量提供了有力工具。

Abstract: Let $X$ be a smooth complex affine variety of dimension $n$, and let $F=(f_1,\ldots,f_r)$ be a tuple of nonzero regular functions on $X$ such that $f:=\prod_{i=1}^r f_i$ is not invertible. We study the zero loci of the Bernstein-Sato ideals $B_F^{\mathbf a}$ for nonnegative integral shifts $\mathbf a$. For a fixed log resolution, every codimension-one irreducible component of $Z(B_F^{\mathbf a})$ is a hyperplane of the form $L_E(\mathbf s)+k_E+c=0$ with $c$ a positive integer. We give a new proof of this result using localized maximal and minimal extensions of relative D-modules. We also prove that $c\leq L_E(\mathbf a)+(n-1-δ_f)L_E(\mathbf 1)-k_E$, where $δ_f=\min\{n-1,α_f\}$ and $α_f$ is the minimal exponent of $f$. The problem of obtaining such an upper bound for arbitrary tuples (in particular, for $r>1$) was raised by Budur, van der Veer, and Van Werde, and the above inequality resolves it. To obtain the upper bound, we compare the diagonal slice of $Z(B_F^{\mathbf 1})$ with the root set of $b_f$. A finite covering by translates, combined with diagonal specialization and the log-resolution description, shows that these sets have the same least and greatest points. Saito's root estimate at their common least point then yields the upper bound. We further establish a divisor-valued formulation of the local index comparison, recovering the detection of monodromy support via monodromy zeta functions and the multivariable A'Campo formula.

</details>


### [207] [The unramified Grothendieck--Serre conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20015)
*Fei Liu*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 证明了在非分歧正则局部环上，约化群概型的通用平凡挠子都是平凡的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该结果旨在推进约化群概型上挠子的局部平凡性理论，特别是处理非分歧正则局部环这一情形，推广相关已知结论。

Method: 通过约化群概型与挠子理论（代数几何/算术几何框架）进行证明。

Result: 证明了：若基环为非分歧正则局部环，则其上约化群概型的通用平凡（generically trivial）挠子必为平凡挠子。

Conclusion: 在非分歧正则局部环上，通用平凡的挠子平凡性成立，为非分歧情形下的约化群挠子理论提供了完整刻画。

Abstract: We prove that a generically trivial torsor under a reductive group scheme over an unramified regular local ring is trivial.

</details>


### [208] [On Logarithmic Poisson and De Rham Cohomology Groups of a Class of Inhomogeneous Divisors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20119)
*Kamtila Kari,Dongho Joseph,Diekouam Fotso Luc Emery*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文研究了仿射平面上一类非齐次自由除子的对数Poisson余调与de Rham余调，显式构造了相关结构并计算了余调群，建立了两种对数余调理论之间的比较同构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 刻画非齐次自由除子的对数Poisson结构与de Rham结构的余调不变量，并将其理论从约化正规交叉除子情形推广到非齐次变形情形。具体而言，作者希望显式计算这类除子对应的对数Poisson余链复形与对数de Rham复形的余调群，并厘清Poisson余调与de Rham余调之间的自然关系。

Method: 研究对象为仿射平面上一类非齐次自由除子，它们是约化正规交叉除子的非齐次变形，并具有Saito意义下对数向量场模的显式基。利用该显式基，作者构造相应的对数Poisson结构，并在Lie-Rinehart代数框架下刻画对数微分1-形式上的诱导Koszul括号。随后确定对数Poisson余链复形并计算其余调；同时借助对数Spencer复形识别对应的对数de Rham复形并计算其余调。

Result: 对该类除子给出了对数Poisson余调群与对数de Rham余调群的显式计算，提供了明确的余调不变量，并展示了这两个理论如何将约化正规交叉除子的相应构造加以推广。在比较方面，证明两种对数余调理论在任意k≠1的度数上自然同构；而在1次，对数de Rham余调群是对数Poisson余调群的一个分裂一维扩张。

Conclusion: 对数Poisson与对数de Rham余调理论可自然推广至仿射平面上的非齐次自由除子，二者在几乎所有的次数上一致（自然同构），仅在1次出现一维分裂扩张的差异，从而给出该类除子的显式余调不变量。

Abstract: We study logarithmic Poisson and de Rham cohomologies associated with a class of inhomogeneous free divisors in the affine plane. These divisors arise as inhomogeneous deformations of reduced normal crossing divisors and admit explicit bases for their modules of logarithmic vector fields in the sense of Saito. Using these bases, we construct the corresponding logarithmic Poisson structures and describe explicitly the induced Koszul bracket on logarithmic differential 1-forms within the framework of Lie-Rinehart algebras. We then determine the logarithmic Poisson cochain complex and compute its cohomology for the class under consideration. Furthermore, by means of the logarithmic Spencer complex, we identify the corresponding logarithmic de Rham complex and compute its cohomology. These computations provide explicit cohomological invariants for the class of inhomogeneous divisors considered and show how logarithmic Poisson and de Rham theories extend the corresponding constructions for reduced normal crossing divisors. In addition, we establish an explicit comparison between the logarithmic cohomological theories: they are naturally isomorphic in any degree $k \neq 1$, whereas in degree 1 the logarithmic de Rham cohomology group is a split one-dimensional extension of the logarithmic Poisson cohomology group.

</details>


### [209] [Classification of Automorphism Groups of Smooth Cubic Threefolds and Fourfolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20403)
*Jie Fu,Shihao Wang,Zhiwei Zheng*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文对光滑三次三维簇与四次簇的自同构群进行分类，并证明分别存在156族光滑三次四次簇与40族光滑三次三维簇具有确定的群作用，且给出各族的作用与定义方程，方法结合表示论（GAP）与格论（SageMath）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光滑三次三维簇与四次簇的自同构群及其带指定群作用的族结构尚缺乏系统分类，需要给出完整清单及具体实现工具。

Method: 结合基于GAP的表示论方法与基于SageMath的格论方法进行代数几何分类计算。

Result: 分类了光滑三次三维簇与四次簇的自同构群；证明光滑三次四次簇有156个连通族、光滑三次三维簇有40个连通族具有指定自同构群作用；给出每族的群作用与定义方程。

Conclusion: 提供了光滑三次三维簇与四次簇自同构群作用族的完整分类与显式数据，表明表示论与格论结合是有效的分类工具。

Abstract: We classify the automorphism groups of smooth cubic threefolds and fourfolds. We also show that there are $156$ (respectively, $40$) connected families of smooth cubic fourfolds (respectively, threefolds) with specified automorphism group action. For each family, the group action and defining equations are also given. The classification combines both representation theory (based on GAP) and lattice theory (based on SageMath).

</details>


### [210] [A Survey on Pham-Brieskorn Varieties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20149)
*Michael Chitayat*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 一篇面向研究生的Pham-Brieskorn簇综述，系统梳理其定义与各类性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Pham-Brieskorn簇是代数几何、复几何与拓扑学交叉处的经典研究对象（如Brieskorn-Pham方程定义的球面同调流形、Milnor纤维化等），但对研究生而言相关文献分散。该综述旨在提供一个教学导向的入门性整理。

Method: 综述性/教学性方法：给出Pham-Brieskorn簇的定义，按主题组织已有的定理与性质，并可能通过例题引导研究生理解。

Result: 由于是综述，未提出新的定理或结果，而是汇总并阐释Pham-Brieskorn簇的已有性质体系。

Conclusion: 该综述为研究生进入Pham-Brieskorn簇研究方向提供系统性的知识框架与入门指引。

Abstract: This is a survey on Pham-Brieskorn varieties and their various properties, targeted for graduate students.

</details>


### [211] [Uniform Rost nilpotence and birational motives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20228)
*David Kumallagov*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文研究Chow motive基变换理想的幂零界，建立了显式幂零界、有效泛降、加权局部块精化及Kok--Zhou检测子的理想化推广。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对域扩张 $E/k$ 下Chow motive $M$ 的基变换理想 $I_E(M)$，已有结果多为逐元素估计或依赖特定几何条件。作者希望获得显式、统一的理想级幂零界，并将分次结构、双有理不变量与检测子方法系统整合，从而在更一般几何设定下量化幂零性与指数。

Method: 引入基变换理想 $I_E(M)$；证明有效泛降，用Rost指数与维数控制指数；在特征零下，利用multilinear Rost滤链的加权局部块精化，把Gille的一致界与Rosenschon--Sawant的逐元素估计升级为理想级统一界；借助Kahn--Sujatha的纯双有理motives描述，得到从双有理到普通Chow motives的定量提升；并将Kok--Zhou检测子从单个对应扩展到整个基变换理想，结合临界精化非分歧上同调的均匀消没给出显式理想幂零界。

Result: 建立若干几何设定下的显式幂零界；证明有效泛降：若 $M$ 是 $h(X)(a)$ 的直和项且 $M_{k(X)}$ 有均匀Rost指数 $s$，则 $M$ 的指数为 $s(\dim X+1)$；由此得到 $2n-2$ 维扭曲Milnor超平面截面的整指数 $2n-1$。特征零下改进Gille的一致界并将Rosenschon--Sawant估计升级为理想级统一界；定量提升结果应用于三维簇，包括双有理于toric模型及具有支撑在曲面上的对角分解的簇；最后获得由临界精化非分歧上同调均匀消没推出的显式理想幂零界。

Conclusion: 基变换理想的幂零性可由均匀Rost指数、滤链分次结构与精化非分歧上同调的消没统一控制；该框架将逐元素结果升级为理想级定量界，并把Kok--Zhou检测子推广到整个基变换理想，为Chow motives的指数与幂零性提供有效工具。

Abstract: For a field extension $E/k$ and a Chow motive $M$, let $I_E(M)=\ker\bigl(\operatorname{End}_k(M)\longrightarrow \operatorname{End}_E(M_E)\bigr)$ be the base-change ideal. We establish explicit nilpotence bounds for these ideals in several geometric settings. We also prove effective generic descent: if $M$ is a summand of $h(X)(a)$ and $M_{k(X)}$ has uniform Rost exponent $s$, then $M$ has exponent $s(\dim X+1)$. This yields the integral exponent $2n-1$ for twisted Milnor hyperplane sections of dimension $2n-2$.
  In characteristic zero, a weighted local-block refinement of the multilinear Rost filtration improves the uniform bounds of Gille and upgrades the elementwise estimates of Rosenschon--Sawant to uniform bounds at the ideal level. Using the Kahn--Sujatha description of pure birational motives, we obtain quantitative lifting results from birational to ordinary Chow motives, with applications to threefolds, including varieties birational to toric models and varieties admitting a decomposition of the diagonal supported on a surface.
  Finally, we extend the Kok--Zhou detector from individual correspondences to entire base-change ideals, obtaining an explicit ideal nilpotence bound from uniform annihilation of the critical refined unramified cohomology groups.

</details>


### [212] [Cartier crystals and dual-constructible $p$-torsion complexes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20287)
*Jefferson Baudin,Kay Rülling*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 在诺特且F-有限的概形上，Cartier晶体上的不动点函子到Kato定义的平坦相对完美site上的层范畴是全忠实的，其本质像恰为作者引入的对偶可构造层构成的子范畴。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Cartier晶体与Kato平坦相对完美site上层的联系，刻画不动点函子的像，从而建立两套理论之间的范畴等价（落到子范畴上）。

Method: 对诺特且F-有限概形，考察Cartier晶体范畴上的不动点函子，将其映到Kato定义的平坦且相对完美site上的层范畴，并引入“对偶可构造层”这一子范畴来刻画其本质像。

Result: 不动点函子是全忠实的，且其本质像恰好等于对偶可构造层所构成的子范畴。

Conclusion: Cartier晶体通过不动点函子可视为Kato相对完美site上对偶可构造层的全子范畴，新引入的对偶可构造性给出了该函子像的精确刻画。

Abstract: We show that given a Noetherian and $F$-finite scheme, the fixed point functor on Cartier crystals is fully faithful to the category of sheaves on the flat and relatively perfect site defined by Kato, and its essential image consists of the subcategory of dual-constructible sheaves, which we introduce.

</details>


### [213] [An exponential Lojasiewicz inequality for semi-Pfaffian sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20361)
*Nicolai Vorobjov*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文推广了Gabrielov版本的Łojasiewicz不等式，将其从本质上的一维情形扩展到非受限半Pfaffian集上的多维情形，并额外刻画了定义域边界上零点邻域内Pfaffian函数的增长速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Gabrielov版本的Łojasiewicz不等式本质上是一维的，且多数Łojasiewicz不等式变体仅描述Pfaffian函数在定义域内部零点集邻域内的增长速率，未涵盖定义域边界上的零点。因此需要将其推广到非受限半Pfaffian集及多维情形。

Method: 通过证明Gabrielov版本Łojasiewicz不等式在非受限半Pfaffian集上的推广形式，将其从一维情形扩展至多维情形，并同时刻画Pfaffian函数在半Pfaffian集X上、不仅在其定义域内零点集邻域中、而且在定义域边界上零点邻域中的增长速率。

Result: 建立了适用于非受限半Pfaffian集的Łojasiewicz不等式推广形式，可同时覆盖定义域内部与边界上零点邻域的增长速率估计，并将Gabrielov的一维结果推广到多维情形。

Conclusion: 本文给出了Łojasiewicz不等式在非受限半Pfaffian集上的多维推广，拓展了Gabrielov的结果，使其能处理定义域边界上的零点并适用于多维设定。

Abstract: We prove a generalization of the version, due to A. Gabrielov, of the Łojasiewicz inequality for not necessarily restricted semi-Pfaffian sets. Unlike the most variants of the Łojasiewicz inequality, it describes the rate of growth of a Pfaffian function on a semi-Pfaffian set $X$ not only in a neighbourhood of its zero set in the domain of $X$ but also in a neighbourhood of zeroes on the boundary of the domain. Gabrielov's version is essentially one-dimensional, we generalize it to a multidimensional case.

</details>


### [214] [On the ACC for Minimal Log Discrepancies for Bounded Generalized Sub-Pairs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20515)
*Weichung Chen,Keng-Hung Steven Lin*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文证明了：在任意维数下，有界广义子对（bounded generalized sub-pairs）的最小对数不 discrepancie（minimal log discrepancies, MLD）的 ACC 猜想成立；同时，具有任意边界（arbitrary boundaries）的有界三维簇也满足该猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最小对数不 discrepancie（MLD）的 ACC 猜想是双有理几何（尤其是森理论/极小模型纲领，MMP）中的核心问题之一，它断言在一类几何对象上，MLD 取值集合满足升链条件（ascending chain condition）。该猜想在一般情形下仍具挑战性。现有结果多局限于特定维数或特定边界类型，作者希望通过引入"有界性"（boundedness）条件，在更大的普遍性下推进该猜想。

Method: 论文采用双有理几何与极小模型纲领框架，围绕正规簇上的广义子对（generalized sub-pairs）及其边界结构展开。核心手段包括：对有界族（bounded families）进行几何有界性控制、利用 MLD 的构造与赋值（valuations）刻画，并结合奇点理论中的不 discrepancie 分析。通过将有界性假设与广义子对（允许系数与奇异结构推广）相结合，将 MLD 的取值离散化，从而验证升链条件。

Result: 取得了两方面结果：(1) 在任意维数下，有界广义子对的 MLD ACC 猜想成立；(2) 在有界三维簇且边界任意（即不限定边界系数或类型）的情形下，MLD ACC 猜想亦成立。后者显著放宽了对边界的限制，扩大了已知成立的适用范围。

Conclusion: 表明"有界性"是支撑 MLD ACC 猜想的关键驱动条件之一。论文以统一方法处理了高维有界广义子对与三维任意边界两类情形，为解决 ACC 猜想提供了新的思路与重要进展，并为后续推广到更一般的有界簇族或放松边界限制的工作奠定基础。

Abstract: We show that the ACC conjecture for minimal log discrepancies holds for both bounded generalized sub-pairs in arbitrary dimension and bounded threefolds with arbitrary boundaries.

</details>


### [215] [Conductor-Discriminant Inequality for Tamely Ramified Cyclic Covers I](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20553)
*Andrew Obus,Padmavathi Srinivasan,Connor Stewart*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文证明了定义在离散赋值域上 \mathbb{Z}/n-覆盖的导子—判别式不等式，将超椭圆曲线上已有结果推广到循环 n 次覆盖情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将前两位作者关于超椭圆曲线的导子—判别式不等式，以及 Ogg、Saito、Liu 等人的工作推广到更一般的 \mathbb{Z}/n-覆盖，并与 Kohls 关于 Jacobian 导子指数的结果建立联系。

Method: 设 K 为赋值环优秀、剩余域完美且特征不整除 n 的离散赋值域。对由 y^n = f(x)、f(x) 属于 O_K[x] 且 n 整除 deg(f) 给出的曲线 X，考虑其 O_K 上的极小正则模型 \mathcal{X}，利用 Artin 导子与判别式（特别是 rad(f) 的判别式）的比较，结合第三位作者工作中的具体计算完成证明。

Result: 证明了 \mathcal{X} 的 Artin 导子的负值不超过 (n-1)v_K(disc(rad(f)))。

Conclusion: 该不等式直接推广了超椭圆曲线情形，并在 f 为首一多项式时强化了 Kohls 的结果，即此类曲线 Jacobian 的导子指数不超过 (n-1)v_K(disc(rad(f)))。

Abstract: We prove conductor-discriminant inequalities for all $\mathbb{Z}/n$-covers of $\mathbb{P}^1$ defined over discretely valued fields $K$ with excellent valuation ring $\mathcal{O}_K$ and perfect residue field of characteristic not dividing $n$, modulo some calculations appearing in work of the third author. Specifically, when such a curve $X$ is given by $y^n = f(x)$ with $f(x) \in\mathcal{O}_K[x]$ and $n\mid\text{deg}(f)$, and if $\mathcal{X}$ is its minimal regular model over $\mathcal{O}_K$, then the negative of the Artin conductor of $\mathcal{X}$ is bounded above by $(n-1)v_K(\text{disc}(\text{rad}(f)))$. This is a direct generalization of previous work of the first two authors on hyperelliptic curves, which in turn generalized work of Ogg, Saito, Liu, and the second author. When $f$ is monic, this strengthens a result of Kohls stating that the conductor exponent of the Jacobian of such a curve is bounded above by $(n-1)v_K(\text{disc}(\text{rad}(f)))$.

</details>


### [216] [The Hurwitz existence problem in prime degree](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20572)
*Jijian Song,Hailin Wen,Zebao Zhang*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 证明球面上任意次数为素数 p 的相容分支数据都可由连通分支覆叠实现，并给出若干 Hurwitz 势与相对 Gromov–Witten 不变量的正性推论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究素数次数分支覆叠的可实现性问题，即判定球面上给定的分支数据是否对应一个连通分支覆叠，并由此推导 Hurwitz 势的支撑性与相对 Gromov–Witten 不变量的非零（正性）结果。

Method: 特三点情形在剩余特征 p 下构造：由 Henrio 矩定理给出互异点矩解，据此对每个给定分拆构造特殊原始尾；正源亏格所需新尾来自矩定理的第二次应用。用对数形变数据连接各尾，嵌入 S_p 中含公共 p 阶正规子群的子群；再用 Wewers 提升定理得到特征零的三点 Galois 覆叠，其对点稳定子的商为次数 p 且具指定三条分支剖面的覆叠；最后用 Edmonds–Kulkarni–Stong 的融合与实现结果推广到任意多个分支值。

Result: 球的任意素数次数相容分支数据可由连通分支覆叠实现（含三点情形）；连通素数次数 Hurwitz 势在 Riemann–Hurwitz 轨迹上具满支撑；P^1 的相应连通相对 Gromov–Witten 不变量均非零；满足维数约束下、偶数完成轮换阶不超过 p 的两相对点非连通扇形严格为正；连通对换扇形严格为正。

Conclusion: 素数次数分支覆叠的可实现性得到完整解决，并由此统一导出 Hurwitz 势满支撑与多类相对 Gromov–Witten 不变量的严格正性。

Abstract: Let $p$ be a prime. We prove that every compatible branch datum of degree $p$ over the sphere is realizable by a connected branched cover. The three-point case is constructed in residue characteristic $p$. Henrio's moment theorem supplies the distinct-point moment solutions from which we construct a special primitive tail for each prescribed partition; a second application underlies the new tail required by a positive source genus. These tails are joined by a logarithmic deformation datum and embedded in one subgroup of $S_p$ containing a common regular subgroup of order $p$. Wewers's lifting theorem produces a three-point Galois cover in characteristic zero. The quotient by a point stabilizer has degree $p$ and the prescribed three ramification profiles. The fusion and realization results of Edmonds--Kulkarni--Stong then give the assertion for an arbitrary number of branch values. As consequences, the connected prime-degree Hurwitz potential has full support on the Riemann--Hurwitz locus, every corresponding connected relative Gromov--Witten invariant of $\mathbf P^1$ is nonzero, the two-relative-point disconnected sector with even completed-cycle orders at most $p$ is strictly positive subject to the dimension constraint, and the connected transposition sector is strictly positive.

</details>


### [217] [Conductor-Discriminant Inequality for Tamely Ramified Cyclic Covers II](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20585)
*Connor Stewart*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文是与 Obus 和 Srinivasan 合作工作的配套论文，为分支除子上重数 2 点 $y$ 的导子-判别式贡献 $\text{cdc}(y)$ 提供必要的技术结果，主要包括其非负性判定以及负值条件下局部环谱的有理二重点刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在与 Obus、Srinivasan 的合作工作中，作者定义了 $\text{cdc}(y)$ 并借助若干关键结果证明了 $X$ 的导子-判别式不等式，推广了 Ogg、Saito、Liu、Srinivasan 及 Obus--Srinivasan 的先前工作。然而该不等式依赖于关于 $\text{cdc}(y)$ 的技术性结论，本文作为配套论文旨在补齐这些必要技术结果。

Method: 设 $K$ 为 Henselian 离散赋值域，整数环 $\mathcal{O}_K$ 优良、剩余域 $k$ 代数闭，$X\to\mathbb{P}^1_K$ 为次数 $n$（与 $k$ 的特征互素）的循环覆盖。对正则 $\mathcal{O}_K$-模型 $\mathcal{Y}$ 在 $K(X)$ 中归一化后的分支除子上重数 2 点 $y$，研究其导子-判别式贡献 $\text{cdc}(y)$；同时引入并研究相关量——导子指数-判别式贡献 $\text{cedc}(y)$。

Result: 证明 $\text{cdc}(y)$ 在除对 $n$ 及 $y$ 处分支除子结构有极强限制条件之外均为非负；若 $\text{cdc}(y)$ 为负，则 $\mathcal{Y}$ 在 $K(X)$ 中归一化下任意位于 $y$ 之上点的完全局部环的谱为有理二重点。此外证明相关量 $\text{cedc}(y)$ 的非负性。

Conclusion: 本文补齐了导子-判别式不等式证明所需的关键技术结果，建立了 $\text{cdc}(y)$ 的非负性判据与负值时的有理二重点结构，并利用 $\text{cedc}(y)$ 的非负性为 Kohls 的一个结果给出了新证明。

Abstract: Let $K$ be a Henselian discretely valued field with excellent ring of integers $\mathcal{O}_K$ and algebraically closed residue field $k$. Let $X\to\mathbb{P}^1_K$ be a cyclic cover of degree $n$ prime to the characteristic of $k$. In joint work with Obus and Srinivasan, we define an integer called the conductor-discriminant contribution $\text{cdc}(y)$ associated to a multiplicity $2$ point $y$ of the branch divisor of the normalization in $K(X)$ of a regular $\mathcal{O}_K$-model $\mathcal{Y}$ of $\mathbb{P}^1_{K}$, and modulo several key results about $\text{cdc}(y)$, we prove a conductor-discriminant inequality for $X$, extending previous work of Ogg, Saito, Liu, Srinivasan, and Obus--Srinivasan. In this companion paper, we supply the necessary technical results for $\text{cdc}(y)$. In particular, we show $\text{cdc}(y)$ is non-negative except under highly restrictive conditions on $n$ and the structure of the branch divisor at $y$. Moreover, if $\text{cdc}(y)$ is negative, we show the spectrum of the complete local ring of any point lying over $y$ under the normalization of $\mathcal{Y}$ in $K(X)$ is a rational double point. Along the way, we show the non-negativity of a related quantity, the conductor exponent-discriminant contribution $\text{cedc}(y)$, which is used in our joint work with Obus and Srinivasan to give a new proof of a result of Kohls.

</details>


### [218] [The Last Picard Rank 1 Double-Mirror Calabi-Yau Pair?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20704)
*Michał Kapustka,Marco Rampazzo,Prajwal Samal*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文研究两族Picard秩为1的Calabi-Yau三维簇（G(2,6)中的33次三维簇族与P^8中的算术Gorenstein 21次三维簇族），建立其一般成员间的几何对应，证明对应三维簇满足经典对偶性，并通过数学GLSM证明二者导出等价，从而验证Miura及Gerhardus-Jockers的猜想，得到一对非双有理的双镜像Calabi-Yau三维簇。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是验证Miura提出的猜想：基于镜像模空间研究，其预测X族成员存在非平凡Fourier-Mukai伴侣，该猜想随后由Gerhardus-Jockers在物理背景下再次提出；同时希望为Picard秩1、非双有理、双镜像Calabi-Yau三维簇的形变族清单增添新成员。

Method: 方法上，先对两族三维簇（Inoue-Ito-Miura构造的G(2,6)中33次三维簇族X，以及Schenck-Stillman-Yuan构造的P^8中算术Gorenstein 21次三维簇族Y）的一般成员建立自然的几何对应；进而证明对应三维簇间满足若干经典对偶性；并借助数学Gauged Linear Sigma Model（GLSM）刻画二者几何关系，据此证明其导出等价；同时从Y族出发推测得到相同的镜像模空间。

Result: 结果表明两族对应的一般三维簇之间存在自然几何对应并满足经典对偶性；通过数学GLSM证明二者导出等价，从而确认了Miura猜想中非平凡Fourier-Mukai伴侣的存在，也回应了Gerhardus-Jockers的物理论述；且从Y族出发可推测得到相同的镜像模空间。

Conclusion: 结论是X与Y族构成一对非双有理、双镜像、Picard秩1的Calabi-Yau三维簇，是此类形变族收录中的新成员，且很可能为该小清单的最后一项。

Abstract: We consider a certain pair of families of Picard rank 1 Calabi-Yau threefolds, that have appeared earlier in mathematical literature in unrelated contexts: the family $\mathcal{X}$ of degree 33 threefolds in $G(2,6)$ (constructed by Inoue-Ito-Miura) and the family $\mathcal{Y}$ of arithmetically Gorenstein degree 21 threefolds in $\mathbb{P}^8$ (constructed by Schenck-Stillman-Yuan). After establishing a natural geometric correspondence between their general members, we go on to show that any pair of corresponding threefolds in these families satisfy certain classical dualities. We moreover discover that their geometries may be related by a mathematical gauged linear sigma model, using which we prove that they are derived equivalent. This settles a conjecture of Miura, who predicted the existence of non-trivial Fourier-Mukai partners to members of $\mathcal{X}$, based on a study of its mirror moduli. This conjecture was also formulated later by Gerhardus-Jockers, in a physical context. In fact, starting from the other family $\mathcal{Y}$, we conjecturally arrive at the same mirror moduli. Thus, such a pair is a new, and quite possibly the last, addition to the small list of deformation families of non-birational, double-mirror Calabi-Yau threefolds having Picard rank 1.

</details>


### [219] [Connecting families of curves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20716)
*Nathan Chen,Robert Lazarsfeld,Federico Moretti*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文研究了光滑射影簇上通过多个一般点的曲线族的亏格与gonality，对非负Kodaira维数情形给出尖锐线性下界，并刻画了gonality的极限值，还探讨了高维连通子簇。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受Kollár–Miyaoka–Mori定理（有理连通簇上任意有限点集位于一条有理曲线上）启发，自然要问：对任意光滑射影簇X，通过其许多一般点的曲线族有何性质。

Method: 当X具有非负Kodaira维数时，建立曲线亏格关于点数与X维数的尖锐线性下界；研究连接族的gonality随点数增长的稳定性；用支配X且为有理连通簇的一般有限覆盖的簇来刻画极限值；并对大次数超曲面计算这些不变量；最后考虑高维连通子簇。

Result: 证明了亏格的尖锐线性下界，推广了Arapura–Archava定理；连接族的最小gonality随点数增加最终稳定，其极限值可由支配X的有理连通簇的一般有限覆盖刻画；对大次数超曲面确定了最小连通亏格随点数与次数变化的联合渐近行为；对高维连通子簇给出典范体积的线性界及超曲面的渐近结果。

Conclusion: 在非负Kodaira维数下，通过多个一般点的曲线族亏格有尖锐线性下界，而gonality最终稳定并有几何刻画；相关结果可推广到高维连通子簇。

Abstract: A theorem of Kollár, Miyaoka, and Mori states that on a rationally connected variety, any finite collection of points lies on a rational curve. Motivated by this, it is natural to ask what one can say about families of curves passing through many general points of an arbitrary smooth projective variety X. When X has nonnegative Kodaira dimension, we establish a sharp linear lower bound for the genus of such curves in terms of the number of points and the dimension of X, generalizing a theorem of Arapura and Archava. By contrast, the least possible gonality of a connecting family eventually stabilizes as the number of points grows. We characterize its limiting value in terms of varieties dominating X that are generically finite covers of rationally connected varieties. As an illustration, we study these invariants for hypersurfaces of large degree, determining in particular the joint asymptotic behavior of the minimal connecting genus as the number of points and the degree vary. Finally, we briefly consider higher-dimensional connecting subvarieties, proving linear bounds for their canonical volumes and computing asymptotic results for hypersurfaces.

</details>


### [220] [Morphism spaces on low degree hypersurfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20783)
*Hrishabh Mishra*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 该论文研究固定次数态射模空间 $\mathrm{Mor}_e(\mathbb P^r, X)$ 的不可约性与维数，证明在 $n$ 足够大时其为预期维数的不可约簇，将 Browning–Yamagishi 关于 $r=2$ 的结果推广到任意 $r$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 态射模空间（射影空间到低次光滑超曲面的固定次数态射）的不可约性与维数是算术几何与有理点计数中的重要问题；已有工作主要处理 $r=2$ 的情形，作者希望推广到更高维定义域 $\mathbb P^r$。

Method: 采用 multiblock Weyl differencing（多块 Weyl 差分）方法，在特征为零或大于 $d$ 的代数闭域上，对 $n\geq 2$、$e\geq 1$、光滑 $d$ 次超曲面 $X\subset \mathbb P^{n-1}$ 进行估计，给出保证不可约性的维数下界条件。

Result: 当 $n> 2^d(d-1)\binom{de+r-1}{r-1}$ 时，$\mathrm{Mor}_e(\mathbb P^r, X)$ 是预期维数的不可约簇。

Conclusion: 该结果把 Browning–Yamagishi 在 $r=2$ 的结论推广至任意 $r$，说明在环境维数足够大时态射模空间具有预期维数与不可约性。

Abstract: For $r> 2$, we study the moduli space parameterising fixed degree morphisms $\mathbb P^r\to X$ where $X$ is a smooth hypersurface of low degree. More precisely, we prove the following result: let $n\geq 2, e\geq 1$ and $X\subset \mathbb P^{n-1}$ a smooth degree $d\geq 2$ hypersurface over an algebraically closed field of characteristic zero or greater than $d$, then $\mathrm{Mor}_e(\mathbb P^r, X)$ is irreducible of the expected dimension if
  \[
  n> 2^d(d-1)\binom{de+r-1}{r-1}.
  \]
  Our result extends the result of Browning-Yamagishi for $r=2$ via multiblock Weyl differencing.

</details>
